All 10 existing groups follow this; circular-import avoidance is why imports sit at module bottom with `# noqa: E402,I001`. The `remediate` group must follow it verbatim.\n65\t- **`claude -p` host = `ClaudeProcess`** (`process.py:72`). Subprocess via `Popen` + process groups (`os.setpgrp`) for whole-tree kill; stdout/stderr redirected to files; lifecycle hooks `on_spawn/on_signal/on_exit`. 18+ callers across sprint/roadmap/prd/eval/swarm establish this as THE established headless pattern — Runner reuses it.\n66\t- **Prompt delivery is stdin, never argv** (`process.py:124-125`, comment referencing commit `4799719`). MAX_ARG_STRLEN (128 KB) no longer applies. This is the mechanical foundation for \"opComment delivered as stdin data inside a CONTROL envelope, never shell-interpolated\" (PA-3).\n67\t- **Env injection is additive-merge, post-copy** (`process.py:155-159`). `os.environ.copy()` → pop nested-session vars → `env.update(env_vars)`. **Convention gap:** there is no \"start from empty + allowlist\" mode. The Runner allowlist (PA-4/AC-3 \"no `GH_TOKEN` in Runner\") requires extending this seam (new param e.g. `env_mode=\"allowlist\"` or `base_env={}`), NOT just passing `env_vars`.\n68\t- **Atomic state writes = tmp-sibling + `os.replace`** (`swarm/state.py:173-175`). NFR-002 contract: every write goes to `path + \".tmp\"` then atomic rename so concurrent readers never see a torn file. The two-phase ledger inherits this durability idiom; adds `O_APPEND`/flock for the append-only JSONL (spec §10), which swarm's whole-file-replace does not cover.\n69\t- **Bounded-counter loop-guard** (`swarm/commands.py:2266-2271`). `iterations` counter + `watch_max_iterations` ceiling + terminal-state-string break. Model for round-count + push-budget short-circuit (spec §9/§10).\n70\t- **Severity-remap is table-driven** (`severity-rubric.md:63-101`): category floor/ceiling → confidence adjustment → diff-locality adjustment → cross-source agreement. H5 must make this structurally unbypassable (wrapper that injects unconditionally).\n72\t- **SoT/sync-dev** (`CLAUDE.md`): source under `src/superclaude/`, never edit/stage `.claude/` (except `settings.json`). New code must pass both.\n74\t\n75\t## FEATURE_ANALYSIS\n76\t\n77\tUser-facing features, each tied to spec evidence and the component inventory (D/R/H/S IDs):\n78\t\n79\t1. **Mention-triggered remediation** — a human collaborator replies `@bot <directive>` to a PR review comment; the bot detects, authorizes, remediates, replies, and (optionally) resolves the thread. *Evidence:* spec §1/§3/§4; PA-1 grammar parser (D4), parent resolver (D6). The `@bot` trigger grammar is a whitelist (D4), not free-form NL.\n80\t2. **Live, per-trigger authorization gate** — only a live event `sender` with `admin|write` collaborator permission (and `type == User`) can authorize; re-checked before every dangerous action. *Evidence:* spec §5; D5. Defends spoofed login, edited mention/parent, fork author, TOCTOU.\n81\t3. **Conservative propose-only default** — autonomy lattice `propose < patch < fix < push < resolve`; effective level = lattice-min(parsed-flag, authz, validation); default `propose`. *Evidence:* spec §8; H2. V1.0 lineage FR-4.4 `needs_human_decision`.\n82\t4. **Injection-contained agent execution** — `opComment` treated as untrusted DATA inside a trusted CONTROL prompt envelope, JSON-encoded, length-capped, stdin-delivered; runs in a non-root ephemeral sandbox with no host-home/`.aienv`/`/config/.claude`/Docker-socket/SSH mounts and deny-by-default egress. *Evidence:* spec §6/§7; R3/R4; stdin precedent `process.py:124-125`.\n83\t5. **Secret-separated push** — Runner gets only minimal Claude/proxy auth (no `GH_TOKEN`); host-side Dispatcher performs git push with a short-lived per-trigger repo+branch-scoped token. *Evidence:* spec §7/§11; H3; reuse seam `process.py:145-160`.\n84\t6. **Idempotent, loop-safe operation** — two-phase intent/outcome JSONL ledger (intent-without-outcome ⇒ RESUME), per-PR push budget, exact-SHA round correlation prevent double-push and infinite loops. *Evidence:* spec §8/§9/§10; H1; swarm idioms `state.py:173-175`, `commands.py:2266-2271`.\n85\t7. **Thread reply + resolve** — bot replies to the originating review thread (`pulls/<N>/comments/<id>/replies`) and resolves it via GraphQL `resolveReviewThread`, matched on `databaseId` (not node id) with pagination. *Evidence:* spec §3/§12; All 10 existing groups follow this; circular-import avoidance is why imports sit at module bottom with `# noqa: E402,I001`. The `remediate` group must follow it verbatim.\n- **`claude -p` host = `ClaudeProcess`** (`process.py:72`). Subprocess via `Popen` + process groups (`os.setpgrp`) for whole-tree kill; stdout/stderr redirected to files; lifecycle hooks `on_spawn/on_signal/on_exit`. 18+ callers across sprint/roadmap/prd/eval/swarm establish this as THE established headless pattern — Runner reuses it.\n- **Prompt delivery is stdin, never argv** (`process.py:124-125`, comment referencing commit `4799719`). MAX_ARG_STRLEN (128 KB) no longer applies. This is the mechanical foundation for \"opComment delivered as stdin data inside a CONTROL envelope, never shell-interpolated\" (PA-3).\n- **Env injection is additive-merge, post-copy** (`process.py:155-159`). `os.environ.copy()` → pop nested-session vars → `env.update(env_vars)`. **Convention gap:** there is no \"start from empty + allowlist\" mode. The Runner allowlist (PA-4/AC-3 \"no `GH_TOKEN` in Runner\") requires extending this seam (new param e.g. `env_mode=\"allowlist\"` or `base_env={}`), NOT just passing `env_vars`.\n- **Atomic state writes = tmp-sibling + `os.replace`** (`swarm/state.py:173-175`). NFR-002 contract: every write goes to `path + \".tmp\"` then atomic rename so concurrent readers never see a torn file. The two-phase ledger inherits this durability idiom; adds `O_APPEND`/flock for the append-only JSONL (spec §10), which swarm's whole-file-replace does not cover.\n- **Bounded-counter loop-guard** (`swarm/commands.py:2266-2271`). `iterations` counter + `watch_max_iterations` ceiling + terminal-state-string break. Model for round-count + push-budget short-circuit (spec §9/§10).\n- **Severity-remap is table-driven** (`severity-rubric.md:63-101`): category floor/ceiling → confidence adjustment → diff-locality adjustment → cross-source agreement. S1 reuses this to map findings to `/sc:troubleshoot` action tiers (spec §17).\n- **Fork-only `gh`** (`CLAUDE.md`): every `gh` call carries `--repo IronbellyOrg/IronClaude`. H5 must make this structurally unbypassable (wrapper that injects unconditionally).\n- **SoT/sync-dev** (`CLAUDE.md`): source under `src/superclaude/`, never edit/stage `.claude/` (except `settings.json`). Skill changes (if any) flow `src/ → make sync-dev`.\n- **UV-only, ruff** (`CLAUDE.md` + memory `reference_make_lint_vs_ci_ruff_format`): `make lint` = `ruff check` only; CI also runs `ruff format --check src/ tests/`. New code must pass both.\n\n## FEATURE_ANALYSIS\n\nUser-facing features, each tied to spec evidence and the component inventory (D/R/H/S IDs):\n\n1. **Mention-triggered remediation** — a human collaborator replies `@bot <directive>` to a PR review comment; the bot detects, authorizes, remediates, replies, and (optionally) resolves the thread. *Evidence:* spec §1/§3/§4; PA-1 grammar parser (D4), parent resolver (D6). The `@bot` trigger grammar is a whitelist (D4), not free-form NL.\n2. **Live, per-trigger authorization gate** — only a live event `sender` with `admin|write` collaborator permission (and `type == User`) can authorize; re-checked before every dangerous action. *Evidence:* spec §5; D5. Defends spoofed login, edited mention/parent, fork author, TOCTOU.\n3. **Conservative propose-only default** — autonomy lattice `propose < patch < fix < push < resolve`; effective level = lattice-min(parsed-flag, authz, validation); default `propose`. *Evidence:* spec §8; H2. V1.0 lineage FR-4.4 `needs_human_decision`.\n4. **Injection-contained agent execution** — `opComment` treated as untrusted DATA inside a trusted CONTROL prompt envelope, JSON-encoded, length-capped, stdin-delivered; runs in a non-root ephemeral sandbox with no host-home/`.aienv`/`/config/.claude`/Docker-socket/SSH mounts and deny-by-default egress. *Evidence:* spec §6/§7; R3/R4; stdin precedent `process.py:124-125`.\n5. **Secret-separated push** — Runner gets only minimal Claude/proxy auth (no `GH_TOKEN`); host-side Dispatcher performs git push with a short-lived per-trigger repo+branch-scoped token. *Evidence:* spec §7/§11; H3; reuse seam `process.py:145-160`.\n6. **Idempotent, loop-safe operation** — two-phase intent/outcome JSONL ledger (intent-without-outcome ⇒ RESUME), per-PR push budget, exact-SHA round correlation prevent double-push and infinite loops. *Evidence:* spec §8/§9/§10; H1; swarm idioms `state.py:173-175`, `commands.py:2266-2271`.\n7. **Thread reply + resolve** — bot replies to the originating review thread (`pulls/<N>/comments/<id>/replies`) and resolves it via GraphQL `resolveReviewThread`, matched on `databaseId` (not node id) with pagination. *Evidence:* spec §3/§12; H4 (greenfield, grep-confirmed absent).\n8. **Production daemon + observability** — systemd-supervised Dispatcher (`Restart=always`, `WatchdogSec`, `ProtectSystem=strict`, `ProtectHome`, `PrivateTmp`), append-only per-trigger audit JSONL, secret redaction (`TOKEN|KEY|AUTH|SECRET` masking + `sys.excepthook` stripping), rate-limit safety (≥30s poll, `Retry-After`, backoff), and alerts (`RemediationLoopDetected`, `AuthzFailureSpike`). *Evidence:* spec §11/§13/§14/§15; S2.\n\n## RECOMMENDED_OUTPUTS\n\nThe PRD pipeline (post-research) should produce:\n\n1. **`prd.md`** — the heavyweight PRD itself: problem/vision, personas (repo maintainer / on-call reviewer), the 7 functional areas (PA-1..PA-7) as FR sections, NFRs (security, isolation, durability, rate-limit), acceptance criteria AC-1..AC-9 (mapped from spec §16), the autonomy lattice + HALT semantics, and the 4 open decisions OD-1..OD-4 surfaced as explicit PRD open questions.\n2. **Component inventory table** — D1–D6 (Dispatcher), R1–R4 (Runner), H1–H5 (host-side), S1–S2 (severity/deploy), each with reuse-vs-build classification and the verified anchor (e.g. R2 → `process.py:72`).\n3. **Trust-boundary / threat-model section** — the bypass-defense matrix (spoofed login, edited mention/parent, fork author, TOCTOU) and the secret-separation three-way split, mapped to AC-1/AC-3/AC-7/AC-9.\n4. **Reuse-seam delta note** — precise statement that `build_env` (`process.py:145`) must gain an allowlist mode; Product surface & user flow (synthesised, spec-grounded)\n\n**Who/what the product is:** a `superclaude remediate` CLI group running headless **outside** any\nClaude session, as an on-prem systemd service. It is not a skill, not a slash command — it\n*spawns* `claude -p`, mirroring `sprint`/`swarm`/`pipeline`. The end user is a **repo maintainer /\non-call reviewer** on the `IronbellyOrg/IronClaude` fork.\n\n**The one user flow that matters (happy path):**\n1. Augment (or a human) leaves a PR **review comment** flagging an issue.\n2. An authorized collaborator **replies** to that comment: `@bot fix --depth deep`.\n3. Dispatcher (polling ≤30–60s) sees the mention, runs the **live authz gate** on the *replier*,\n   claims the trigger in the ledger, resolves the **parent** comment as `opComment`, parses the\n   whitelisted flags.\n4. A one-shot sandboxed Runner checks out PR-head, runs `/sc:troubleshoot` against\n   `OP_COMMENT_JSON.body` (as DATA), validates, emits a diff (propose) or a sandbox-branch commit (fix).\n5. Dispatcher pushes host-side with a short-lived token, replies to the thread with the summary +\n   SHA, and (at `resolve` level) resolves the thread.\n\n**The control surface the user actually touches** is the **@-mention grammar** (D4): a tiny\nwhitelist — autonomy level (`propose|patch|fix|push|resolve`), `--depth`, `--scope`, `--rounds`.\nEverything else is config/operator-set. **Default with no flag = `propose`** (safest). This is the\nentire UX: one comment, a few whitelisted tokens.\n\n**Critical UX safety property:** the *replier* is the sole authority; the *parent author* supplies\nonly data. A `read`-permission user mentioning the bot gets a polite ack-reject, zero action (AC-1).\n\n**Key Takeaways**\n- The product's \"interface\" is a 4-token comment grammar + a systemd service; there is no GUI,\n  no web surface, no new slash command for end users.\n- The conservative default (propose-only) is the dominant UX decision — it must be impossible to\n  reach `push` without an explicit flag AND write-permission AND passing validation (lattice-min).\n\n---\n\n## 4. Integration opportunities & touchpoints\n\n- **`offer-pr-review.sh` hook** **[CODE-VERIFIED, refines seed-brief]** — exists at\n  `src/superclaude/hooks/scripts/offer-pr-review.sh` (canonical) and is **registered project-local\n  in `.claude/settings.json`** as a PostToolUse Bash hook. The seed-brief's \"not wired into\n  hooks.json\" is precise only about the **distributable** `src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json` (where\n  it is indeed absent — a known SoT drift flagged in `.dev/reviews/pr-67-.../auggie-parsed-C.json`).\n  *Integration opportunity:* V2.0 could surface the mention-trigger path via this existing\n  PR-review-offer touchpoint, but the PRD should **not** depend on it being distributed until the\n  hooks.json drift is reconciled. The PRD can cite this as an *existing,\n  code-enforced cultural invariant*, not a new rule it invents.\n- **CLI-group siblings** — `cli/prd/` (18 modules) and `cli/swarm/` are the structural template for\n  decomposing Dispatcher (D1–D6) / Runner (R1–R4) / host-side (H1–H5) into sibling modules under\n  `cli/remediate/`. The registration test home is `tests/cli/remediate/` (ABSENT today; mirror\n  `tests/cli/test_cli_registration.py`).\n- **`~/.aienv` proxy contract** (per memory `feedback_aienv_only_proxy_contract`) — the Runner's\n  Anthropic auth must come from the `:4000/cli` proxy base + `T2Model*` model ids only; this is the\n  **only** credential class allowed into the sandbox env (everything GitHub stays host-side).\n\n---\n\n## 5. Since it is the\n  **default**, the Runner inherits it for free — but the PRD must make explicit that this default is\n  only safe **because** of the sandbox boundary (no host mounts, deny-egress); outside that boundary\n  the same default would be dangerous. Product surface & user flow (synthesised, spec-grounded)\n\n**Who/what the product is:** a `superclaude remediate` CLI group running headless **outside** any\nClaude session, as an on-prem systemd service. It is not a skill, not a slash command — it\n*spawns* `claude -p`, mirroring `sprint`/`swarm`/`pipeline`. The end user is a **repo maintainer /\non-call reviewer** on the `IronbellyOrg/IronClaude` fork.\n\n**The one user flow that matters (happy path):**\n1. Augment (or a human) leaves a PR **review comment** flagging an issue.\n2. An authorized collaborator **replies** to that comment: `@bot fix --depth deep`.\n3. Dispatcher (polling ≤30–60s) sees the mention, runs the **live authz gate** on the *replier*,\n   claims the trigger in the ledger, resolves the **parent** comment as `opComment`, parses the\n   whitelisted flags.\n4. A one-shot sandboxed Runner checks out PR-head, runs `/sc:troubleshoot` against\n   `OP_COMMENT_JSON.body` (as DATA), validates, emits a diff (propose) or a sandbox-branch commit (fix).\n5. Dispatcher pushes host-side with a short-lived token, replies to the thread with the summary +\n   SHA, and (at `resolve` level) resolves the thread.\n\n**The control surface the user actually touches** is the **@-mention grammar** (D4): a tiny\nwhitelist — autonomy level (`propose|patch|fix|push|resolve`), `--depth`, `--scope`, `--rounds`.\nEverything else is config/operator-set. **Default with no flag = `propose`** (safest). This is the\nentire UX: one comment, a few whitelisted tokens.\n\n**Critical UX safety property:** the *replier* is the sole authority; the *parent author* supplies\nonly data. A `read`-permission user mentioning the bot gets a polite ack-reject, zero action (AC-1).\n\n**Key Takeaways**\n- The product's \"interface\" is a 4-token comment grammar + a systemd service; there is no GUI,\n  no web surface, no new slash command for end users.\n- The conservative default (propose-only) is the dominant UX decision — it must be impossible to\n  reach `push` without an explicit flag AND write-permission AND passing validation (lattice-min).\n\n---\n\n## 4. Dispatcher (polling ≤30–60s) sees the mention, runs the **live authz gate** on the *replier*,","+   claims the trigger in the ledger, resolves the **parent** comment as `opComment`, parses the","+   whitelisted flags.","+4. A one-shot sandboxed Runner checks out PR-head, runs `/sc:troubleshoot` against","+   `OP_COMMENT_JSON.body` (as DATA), validates, emits a diff (propose) or a sandbox-branch commit (fix).","+5. Dispatcher pushes host-side with a short-lived token, replies to the thread with the summary +","+   SHA, and (at `resolve` level) resolves the thread.","+","+**The control surface the user actually touches** is the **@-mention grammar** (D4): a tiny","+whitelist — autonomy level (`propose|patch|fix|push|resolve`), `--depth`, `--scope`, `--rounds`.","+Everything else is config/operator-set. **Default with no flag = `propose`** (safest). This is the","+entire UX: one comment, a few whitelisted tokens.","+","+**Critical UX safety property:** the *replier* is the sole authority; the *parent author* supplies","+only data. A `read`-permission user mentioning the bot gets a polite ack-reject, zero action (AC-1).","+","+**Key Takeaways**","+- The product's \"interface\" is a 4-token comment grammar + a systemd service; there is no GUI,","+  no web surface, no new slash command for end users.","+- The conservative default (propose-only) is the dominant UX decision — it must be impossible to","+  reach `push` without an explicit flag AND write-permission AND passing validation (lattice-min).","+","+---","+","+## 4. Product surface & user flow (synthesised, spec-grounded)\n\n**Who/what the product is:** a `superclaude remediate` CLI group running headless **outside** any\nClaude session, as an on-prem systemd service. It is not a skill, not a slash command — it\n*spawns* `claude -p`, mirroring `sprint`/`swarm`/`pipeline`. The end user is a **repo maintainer /\non-call reviewer** on the `IronbellyOrg/IronClaude` fork.\n\n**The one user flow that matters (happy path):**\n1. Augment (or a human) leaves a PR **review comment** flagging an issue.\n2. An authorized collaborator **replies** to that comment: `@bot fix --depth deep`.\n3. Dispatcher (polling ≤30–60s) sees the mention, runs the **live authz gate** on the *replier*,\n   claims the trigger in the ledger, resolves the **parent** comment as `opComment`, parses the\n   whitelisted flags.\n4. A one-shot sandboxed Runner checks out PR-head, runs `/sc:troubleshoot` against\n   `OP_COMMENT_JSON.body` (as DATA), validates, emits a diff (propose) or a sandbox-branch commit (fix).\n5. Dispatcher pushes host-side with a short-lived token, replies to the thread with the summary +\n   SHA, and (at `resolve` level) resolves the thread.\n\n**The control surface the user actually touches** is the **@-mention grammar** (D4): a tiny\nwhitelist — autonomy level (`propose|patch|fix|push|resolve`), `--depth`, `--scope`, `--rounds`.\nEverything else is config/operator-set. **Default with no flag = `propose`** (safest). This is the\nentire UX: one comment, a few whitelisted tokens.\n\n**Critical UX safety property:** the *replier* is the sole authority; the *parent author* supplies\nonly data. A `read`-permission user mentioning the bot gets a polite ack-reject, zero action (AC-1).\n\n**Key Takeaways**\n- The product's \"interface\" is a 4-token comment grammar + a systemd service; there is no GUI,\n  no web surface, no new slash command for end users.\n- The conservative default (propose-only) is the dominant UX decision — it must be impossible to\n  reach `push` without an explicit flag AND write-permission AND passing validation (lattice-min).\n\n---\n\n## 4. Integration opportunities & touchpoints\n\n- **`offer-pr-review.sh` hook** **[CODE-VERIFIED, refines seed-brief]** — exists at\n  `src/superclaude/hooks/scripts/offer-pr-review.sh` (canonical) and is **registered project-local\n  in `.claude/settings.json`** as a PostToolUse Bash hook. The seed-brief's \"not wired into\n  hooks.json\" is precise only about the **distributable** `src/superclaude/hooks/hooks.json` (where\n  it is indeed absent — a known SoT drift flagged in `.dev/reviews/pr-67-.../auggie-parsed-C.json`).\n  *Integration opportunity:* V2.0 could surface the mention-trigger path via this existing\n  PR-review-offer touchpoint, but the PRD should **not** depend on it being distributed until the\n  hooks.json drift is reconciled. The registration test home is `tests/cli/remediate/` (ABSENT today; mirror\n  `tests/cli/test_cli_registration.py`).\n- **`~/.aienv` proxy contract** (per memory `feedback_aienv_only_proxy_contract`) — the Runner's\n  Anthropic auth must come from the `:4000/cli` proxy base + `T2Model*` model ids only; this is the\n  **only** credential class allowed into the sandbox env (everything GitHub stays host-side).\n\n---\n\n## 5. Edge cases & divergences worth flagging to the PRD\n\n- **Seed-brief vs merged-requirements injection model [CODE-CONTRADICTED — important evolution].**\n  The seed-brief (line 21, 32) describes the execution primitive literally as\n  `/sc:troubleshoot \"${opComment}\" --depth deep --fix` — i.e. **shell-interpolating** opComment.\n  The adversarial merge **hardened this away**: merged-requirements §6 states opComment is\n  *\"**never** interpolated as `/sc:troubleshoot \\\"${opComment}\\\"`\"* and is instead JSON-encoded in a\n  CONTROL/DATA envelope delivered via stdin. **The PRD MUST use the §6 envelope form**, never the\n  seed-brief's literal interpolation — citing the seed verbatim would re-introduce the exact\n  injection vuln (SC-2/AC-3) the design exists to prevent.
